  New: src/banking/outcome.ts (pure, no I/O)

  // Decision table for a transfer intent. See 
  submission/03-fixes/01-transfer-integrity.md.
  // Only a verified bank fact yields completed or not_executed; anything else 
  stays pending.
  export type Reason = 'insufficient_funds' | 'refused' | 'not_sent' |
  'not_recorded';
  export type Step =
    | { next: 'completed' }
    | { next: 'not_executed'; reason: Reason }
    | { next: 'pending_verification'; anomaly: boolean }
    | { next: 'verify' }
    | { next: 'resend' };
  // status is null when no readable answer arrived.
  export function afterSend(status: number | null): Step {
    if (status !== null && status >= 200 && status < 300) return { next:
  'completed' };
    if (status === 409) return { next: 'pending_verification', anomaly: true };
    if (status === 422) return { next: 'not_executed', reason:
  'insufficient_funds' };
    if (status !== null && status >= 400 && status < 500)
      return { next: 'not_executed', reason: 'refused' };
    return { next: 'verify' };
  }
  // first: right after the first send, when resending the same reference is 
  safe.
  // unsettled: the bank may still be processing a send, so an absent operation 
  proves nothing.
  // settled: long after the last send, when an absent operation means it never 
  happened.
  export type LookupPhase = 'first' | 'unsettled' | 'settled';
  export function afterLookup(status: number | null, matches: boolean, phase:
  LookupPhase): Step {
    if (status === 200)
      return matches ? { next: 'completed' } : { next: 'pending_verification',
  anomaly: true };
    if (status === 404 && phase === 'first') return { next: 'resend' };
    if (status === 404 && phase === 'settled')
      return { next: 'not_executed', reason: 'not_recorded' };
    return { next: 'pending_verification', anomaly: false };
  }
  export type Wording =
    | Reason
    | 'completed'
    | 'pending_verification'
    | 'resolved_completed'
    | 'resolved_not_executed'
    | 'identical_pending'
    | 'identical_completed';
  export type Details = { amount: string; recipient: string; account: string;
  reference: string };
  export function customerMessage(kind: Wording, d: Details) {
    const transfer = `your transfer of ${d.amount} to ${d.recipient}`;
    switch (kind) {
      case 'completed':
        return `Your transfer of ${d.amount} to ${d.recipient} is complete. The
  bank has confirmed it. Reference: ${d.reference}.`;
      case 'insufficient_funds':
        return `Your transfer was not made: there is not enough money in 
  ${d.account}. No money has left your account.`;
      case 'refused':
        return 'Your transfer was not made: the bank did not accept these 
  details. No money has left your account.';
      case 'not_sent':
        return 'We could not reach the bank, so your transfer was not sent. No 
  money has left your account. You can try again in a few minutes.';
      case 'pending_verification':
        return `We sent ${transfer}, but the bank has not confirmed it yet, so
  we cannot tell you whether it went through. Please do not send it again: you
  could pay twice. We will check with the bank (reference ${d.reference}) when
  you next open your accounts or ask us. If you need an answer now, ask for a
  person to review it.`;
      case 'resolved_completed':
        return `We have checked with the bank: ${transfer} was completed, once.
  Reference: ${d.reference}.`;
      case 'not_recorded':
      case 'resolved_not_executed':
        return `We have checked with the bank: ${transfer} was not made and no
  money has left your account. If you still want to send it, you can start it
  again.`;
      case 'identical_pending':
        return `You already have an identical transfer being verified 
  (${d.amount} to ${d.recipient}). We have not sent a second one, so you cannot
  pay twice.`;
      case 'identical_completed':
        return `We have checked with the bank: your earlier transfer of 
  ${d.amount} to ${d.recipient} was completed, once. Reference: ${d.reference}.
  We have not sent a second one. If you want to send another, ask again.`;
    }
  }

  Rewritten: src/banking/dispatch.ts

  import { bankRequest, BankError } from './client';
  import { afterLookup, afterSend, type LookupPhase, type Reason } from
  './outcome';
  import type { Operation, TransferInput } from '../types';
  export type Outcome =
    | { state: 'completed'; operation: Operation }
    | { state: 'not_executed'; reason: Reason; error: string }
    | { state: 'pending_verification'; anomaly: boolean };
  type Answer = { status: number; operation?: Operation; error?: string };
  async function ask(userId: string, path: string, method = 'GET', body?:
  unknown): Promise<Answer> {
    try {
      return { status: 200, operation: await bankRequest<Operation>(userId,
  path, method, body) };
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof BankError)) throw e;
      return { status: e.status, error: e.message };
    }
  }
  const same = (op: Operation, input: TransferInput) =>
    op.fromAccountId === input.fromAccountId &&
    op.toAccountId === input.toAccountId &&
    op.amountCents === input.amountCents &&
    op.concept === input.concept;
  // Read-only: asks the bank what it recorded under this reference. Never moves
  money.
  export async function verifyTransfer(
    userId: string,
    input: TransferInput,
    reference: string,
    phase: LookupPhase,
  ): Promise<Outcome | 'resend'> {
    const found = await ask(userId,
  `/v1/operations/${encodeURIComponent(reference)}`);
    const step = afterLookup(found.status, !!found.operation &&
  same(found.operation, input), phase);
    if (step.next === 'completed') return { state: 'completed', operation:
  found.operation! };
    if (step.next === 'not_executed')
      return {
        state: 'not_executed',
        reason: step.reason,
        error: 'The bank has no record of this operation.',
      };
    if (step.next === 'resend') return 'resend';
    return { state: 'pending_verification', anomaly: 'anomaly' in step &&
  step.anomaly };
  }
  // One reference per intent, for life: the bank's idempotency key makes a 
  resend harmless.
  export async function dispatchTransfer(
    userId: string,
    input: TransferInput,
    reference: string,
  ): Promise<Outcome> {
    for (let phase: LookupPhase = 'first'; ; phase = 'unsettled') {
      const sent = await ask(userId, '/v1/transfers', 'POST', { ...input,
  reference });
      const step = afterSend(sent.status);
      if (step.next === 'completed') return { state: 'completed', operation:
  sent.operation! };
      if (step.next === 'not_executed')
        return {
          state: 'not_executed',
          reason: step.reason,
          error: sent.error || 'Bank service error.',
        };
      if (step.next === 'pending_verification')
        return { state: 'pending_verification', anomaly: step.anomaly };
      const verified = await verifyTransfer(userId, input, reference, phase);
      if (verified !== 'resend') return verified;
    }
  }

  Rewritten: src/banking/actions.ts

  import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
  import { z } from 'zod';
  import { appDb } from '../db';
  import { HttpError } from '../auth';
  import { person } from '../people';
  import { authorizeTransfer } from './authorization';
  import { bankRequest, BankError } from './client';
  import { dispatchTransfer, verifyTransfer, type Outcome } from './dispatch';
  import { customerMessage, type Wording } from './outcome';
  import { recordEvent } from '../telemetry';
  import type { Account, ActionResult, ToolContext, TransferInput } from
  '../types';
  export const transferSchema = z
    .object({
      fromAccountId: z.string().min(1),
      toAccountId: z.string().min(1),
      amountCents: z.number().int().positive().max(10000000),
      concept: z.string().max(200),
    })
    .strict();
  type Intent = {
    id: string;
    user_id: string;
    payload: string;
    status: string;
    bank_reference: string | null;
    operation_id: string | null;
    created_at: string;
  };
  // Before this, the bank may still be processing a send: an absent operation 
  proves nothing.
  const SETTLE_MS = 30000;
  const money = (cents: number) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IE', { style: 'currency', currency: 'EUR'
  }).format(cents / 100);
  // Within one customer message, identical details are one intent however often
  the tool is called.
  export function intentKey(args: unknown) {
    const parsed = transferSchema.safeParse(args);
    return createHash('sha256')
      .update(JSON.stringify(parsed.success ? parsed.data : args))
      .digest('hex')
      .slice(0, 24);
  }
  async function describe(userId: string, input: TransferInput, reference:
  string) {
    const details = {
      amount: money(input.amountCents),
      recipient: input.toAccountId,
      account: input.fromAccountId,
      reference,
    };
    try {
      const [accounts, contacts] = await Promise.all([
        bankRequest<Account[]>(userId, '/v1/accounts'),
        bankRequest<(Account & { name?: string })[]>(userId, '/v1/contacts'),
      ]);
      const to = contacts.find((c) => c.id === input.toAccountId);
      if (to) details.recipient = `${to.name || to.id} (${to.label})`;
      details.account = accounts.find((a) => a.id ===
  input.fromAccountId)?.label || details.account;
    } catch {}
    return details;
  }
  // Stores the outcome and builds what the customer is told.
  async function settle(
    ctx: ToolContext,
    intent: Intent,
    input: TransferInput,
    outcome: Outcome,
    wording?: Wording,
  ): Promise<ActionResult> {
    const db = appDb(),
      reference = intent.bank_reference!,
      base = { intentId: intent.id, reference },
      details = await describe(ctx.userId, input, reference);
    if (outcome.state === 'completed') {
      db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=?,operation_id=?,error=NULL WHERE 
  id=?').run(
        'completed',
        outcome.operation.id,
        intent.id,
      );
      recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.completed', {
        status: 'completed',
        operation: outcome.operation,
        intentId: intent.id,
      });
      return {
        status: 'completed',
        operation: outcome.operation,
        ...base,
        message: customerMessage(wording ?? 'completed', details),
      };
    }
    if (outcome.state === 'not_executed') {
      db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=?,error=? WHERE id=?').run(
        'failed',
        outcome.error,
        intent.id,
      );
      recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.failed', {
        status: 'failed',
        error: outcome.error,
        intentId: intent.id,
      });
      return {
        status: 'failed',
        error: outcome.error,
        reason: outcome.reason,
        ...base,
        message: customerMessage(wording ?? outcome.reason, details),
      };
    }
    db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=?,error=NULL WHERE id=?').run(
      'pending_verification',
      intent.id,
    );
    recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.pending', { status: 'pending_verification',
  intentId: intent.id });
    return {
      status: 'pending_verification',
      needsReview: outcome.anomaly,
      ...base,
      message: customerMessage(wording ?? 'pending_verification', details),
    };
  }
  // Asks the bank about an intent whose outcome is still unknown. Never sends 
  money.
  async function recheck(ctx: ToolContext, intent: Intent, input: TransferInput,
  identical = false) {
    const settled = Date.now() - Date.parse(intent.created_at) > SETTLE_MS;
    let outcome: Outcome = { state: 'pending_verification', anomaly: false };
    try {
      const verified = await verifyTransfer(
        ctx.userId,
        input,
        intent.bank_reference!,
        settled ? 'settled' : 'unsettled',
      );
      if (verified !== 'resend') outcome = verified;
    } catch {}
    const wording: Wording =
      outcome.state === 'completed'
        ? identical
          ? 'identical_completed'
          : 'resolved_completed'
        : outcome.state === 'not_executed'
          ? 'resolved_not_executed'
          : identical
            ? 'identical_pending'
            : 'pending_verification';
    return settle(ctx, intent, input, outcome, wording);
  }
  export async function transferMoney(ctx: ToolContext, args: unknown):
  Promise<ActionResult> {
    if (person(ctx.userId)?.role !== 'customer')
      throw new HttpError(403, 'A customer account is required.');
    const input = transferSchema.parse(args),
      db = appDb(),
      payload = JSON.stringify(input);
    const byId = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM intents WHERE id=?');
    const previous = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent | undefined;
    if (previous && (previous.user_id !== ctx.userId || previous.payload !==
  payload))
      throw new HttpError(409, 'This intent belongs to a different payload.');
    // Same intent again: a completed one returns its verified result, a pending
  one is rechecked.
    if (previous?.status === 'completed' && previous.operation_id) {
      const reference = previous.bank_reference!;
      const found = await verifyTransfer(ctx.userId, input, reference,
  'unsettled').catch(() => null);
      return {
        status: 'completed',
        operation:
          found && found !== 'resend' && found.state === 'completed'
            ? found.operation
            : { id: previous.operation_id, userId: ctx.userId, reference,
  ...input, status: 'completed' },
        intentId: previous.id,
        reference,
        message: customerMessage('completed', await describe(ctx.userId, input,
  reference)),
      };
    }
    if (previous?.status === 'pending_verification') return recheck(ctx,
  previous, input);
    if (!previous) {
      // D8: an identical request while one is unverified is reconciled first, 
  never sent blindly.
      const pending = db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM intents WHERE user_id=? AND payload=? AND 
  status='pending_verification' ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 1",
        )
        .get(ctx.userId, payload) as Intent | undefined;
      if (pending) {
        const result = await recheck(ctx, pending, input, true);
        if (result.status !== 'failed') return result;
      }
      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO intents 
  VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(
        ctx.intentId,
        ctx.userId,
        ctx.conversationId,
        ctx.runId,
        payload,
        'created',
        randomUUID(),
        null,
        null,
        new Date().toISOString(),
      );
    }
    let intent = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent;
    if (!intent.bank_reference) {
      db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET bank_reference=? WHERE 
  id=?').run(randomUUID(), intent.id);
      intent = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent;
    }
    try {
      const permission = await authorizeTransfer(ctx, input);
      if (permission) return permission;
    } catch (e) {
      // The bank could not be reached before sending: nothing left the 
  application.
      if (e instanceof BankError && e.status >= 500)
        return settle(ctx, intent, input, { state: 'not_executed', reason:
  'not_sent', error: e.message });
      throw e;
    }
    db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=? WHERE id=?').run('processing',
  intent.id);
    recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.started', { status: 'processing', input,
  intentId: intent.id });
    let outcome: Outcome;
    try {
      outcome = await dispatchTransfer(ctx.userId, input,
  intent.bank_reference!);
    } catch {
      // Once sent, an unexpected error proves nothing about the bank.
      outcome = { state: 'pending_verification', anomaly: false };
    }
    return settle(ctx, intent, input, outcome);
  }

  import test from 'node:test';
  import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
  import { afterLookup, afterSend, customerMessage, type Wording } from
  '../src/banking/outcome';
  // One row per line of the state table in 
  submission/03-fixes/01-transfer-integrity.md.
  test('a send answer settles only on a verified bank fact', () => {
    assert.deepEqual(afterSend(200), { next: 'completed' });
    assert.deepEqual(afterSend(422), { next: 'not_executed', reason:
  'insufficient_funds' });
    for (const status of [400, 401, 403])
      assert.deepEqual(afterSend(status), { next: 'not_executed', reason:
  'refused' });
    assert.deepEqual(afterSend(409), { next: 'pending_verification', anomaly:
  true });
    for (const status of [500, 502, 503, 504, null])
      assert.deepEqual(afterSend(status), { next: 'verify' });
  });
  test('a lookup confirms, allows one resend, or leaves the intent pending', () 
  => {
    for (const phase of ['first', 'unsettled', 'settled'] as const) {
      assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(200, true, phase), { next: 'completed' });
      assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(200, false, phase), { next:
  'pending_verification', anomaly: true });
      for (const status of [500, 503, 504, null])
        assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(status, false, phase), {
          next: 'pending_verification',
          anomaly: false,
        });
    }
    assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(404, false, 'first'), { next: 'resend' });
    assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(404, false, 'unsettled'), {
      next: 'pending_verification',
      anomaly: false,
    });
    assert.deepEqual(afterLookup(404, false, 'settled'), {
      next: 'not_executed',
      reason: 'not_recorded',
    });
  });
  test('uncertainty never produces a failure or a completion', () => {
    const uncertain = [
      ...[500, 503, 504, null].map((s) => afterSend(s)),
      ...[500, 503, 504, null].map((s) => afterLookup(s, false, 'settled')),
    ];
    for (const step of uncertain) assert.ok(!['completed',
  'not_executed'].includes(step.next));
  });
  test('customer wording never says failed, and pending says not to repeat the 
  payment', () => {
    const details = { amount: '€10.00', recipient: 'Bruno Vidal', account:
  'Aurora account', reference: 'ref-1' };
    const kinds: Wording[] = [
      'completed', 'insufficient_funds', 'refused', 'not_sent', 'not_recorded',
  'pending_verification',
      'resolved_completed', 'resolved_not_executed', 'identical_pending',
  'identical_completed',
    ];
    for (const kind of kinds) assert.doesNotMatch(customerMessage(kind,
  details), /fail/i);
    const pending = customerMessage('pending_verification', details);
    assert.match(pending, /do not send it again/);
    assert.match(pending, /ref-1/);
  });

  What I need you to check

  1. How I read your decision 4 (D8). If the lookup resolves the earlier
     identical transfer as completed, this call tells the customer so and does
     not send the new one. They can ask again, and that next request is a fresh
     intent. Until Fix 2 adds confirmation, this is what stops "try again" from
     paying twice. If the earlier one resolves as not executed, the new one is
     sent normally.
  2. One new message, not yet approved (identical_completed): "We have checked
     with the bank: your earlier transfer of {amount} to {recipient} was
     completed, once. Reference: {reference}. We have not sent a second one. If
     you want to send another, ask again."
  3. 30-second settle window. A lookup 404 only counts as "not executed" once
     the intent is 30 seconds old; before that it stays pending. I added this to
     the design file's table because a bank could still be processing a send
     that timed out.
  4. Repeating a failed intent re-sends it with the same reference. A verified
     refusal had no effect, so the bank re-evaluates it (for example, funds are
     now sufficient), and it can still execute at most once.
  5. Two extra bank reads per transfer result, to show the recipient's name and
     the account label in the message. If they fail, the message falls back to
     account ids.
  6. Unverified parts. I have not run any of this. The plan after your OK is:
     write, prettier, tsc, npm test, then npm run repro -- --label after-fix-1a,
     and show you the table before committing.

  Waiting for your OK before writing.

✻ Churned for 4m 30s · done 18:52
❯ Approved, with two changes:                                                   
                                                                                
  1. Settle window: measure it from the last send, not from intent creation.    
  Example: an intent created an hour ago, refused for insufficient funds,       
  re-sent now, times out and is rechecked a few seconds later; a 404 would      
  wrongly count as "settled" and become not_executed although the send was      
  seconds ago. Store the time of the last send (for example a last_sent_at      
  column, with a safe migration for existing databases) and use it. Add a unit  
  test for this case.                                                           
  2. identical_completed wording: "We have checked with the bank: your earlier  
  transfer of {amount} to {recipient} was completed, once. Reference:           
  {reference}. We have not sent it again. If you want to make a second,         
  separate payment, ask us again and we will send it."                          
                                                                                
  Then go ahead as planned: write, prettier, tsc, npm test, and npm run repro   
  -- --label after-fix-1a. Show me the table before committing. If any case     
  that should pass still fails, stop and diagnose before changing anything      
  else.

      111 +  recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.pending', { status: 'pending_veri
          +fication', intentId: intent.id });                            
      112 +  return {                                                    
      113 +    status: 'pending_verification',                           
      114 +    needsReview: outcome.anomaly,                             
      115 +    ...base,                                                  
      116 +    message: customerMessage(wording ?? 'pending_verification'
          +, details),                                                   
      117 +  };                                                          
      118  }
      119 +// Asks the bank about an intent whose outcome is still unknow
          +n. Never sends money.                                         
      120 +async function recheck(ctx: ToolContext, intent: Intent, input
          +: TransferInput, identical = false) {                         
      121 +  let outcome: Outcome = { state: 'pending_verification', anom
          +aly: false };                                                 
      122 +  try {                                                       
      123 +    const verified = await verifyTransfer(                    
      124 +      ctx.userId,                                             
      125 +      input,                                                  
      126 +      intent.bank_reference!,                                 
      127 +      lookupPhase(intent.last_sent_at ?? intent.created_at),  
      128 +    );                                                        
      129 +    if (verified !== 'resend') outcome = verified;            
      130 +  } catch {}                                                  
      131 +  const wording: Wording =                                    
      132 +    outcome.state === 'completed'                             
      133 +      ? identical                                             
      134 +        ? 'identical_completed'                               
      135 +        : 'resolved_completed'                                
      136 +      : outcome.state === 'not_executed'                      
      137 +        ? 'resolved_not_executed'                             
      138 +        : identical                                           
      139 +          ? 'identical_pending'                               
      140 +          : 'pending_verification';                           
      141 +  return settle(ctx, intent, input, outcome, wording);        
      142 +}                                                             
      143 +export async function transferMoney(ctx: ToolContext, args: un
          +known): Promise<ActionResult> {                               
      144 +  if (person(ctx.userId)?.role !== 'customer')                
      145 +    throw new HttpError(403, 'A customer account is required.'
          +);                                                            
      146 +  const input = transferSchema.parse(args),                   
      147 +    db = appDb(),                                             
      148 +    payload = JSON.stringify(input);                          
      149 +  const byId = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM intents WHERE id=?');
      150 +  const previous = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent | undefine
          +d;                                                            
      151 +  if (previous && (previous.user_id !== ctx.userId || previous
          +.payload !== payload))                                        
      152 +    throw new HttpError(409, 'This intent belongs to a differe
          +nt payload.');                                                
      153 +  // Same intent again: a completed one returns its verified r
          +esult, a pending one is rechecked.                            
      154 +  if (previous?.status === 'completed' && previous.operation_i
          +d) {                                                          
      155 +    const reference = previous.bank_reference!;               
      156 +    const found = await verifyTransfer(ctx.userId, input, refe
          +rence, 'unsettled').catch(() => null);                        
      157 +    return {                                                  
      158 +      status: 'completed',                                    
      159 +      operation:                                              
      160 +        found && found !== 'resend' && found.state === 'comple
          +ted'                                                          
      161 +          ? found.operation                                   
      162 +          : {                                                 
      163 +              id: previous.operation_id,                      
      164 +              userId: ctx.userId,                             
      165 +              reference,                                      
      166 +              ...input,                                       
      167 +              status: 'completed',                            
      168 +            },                                                
      169 +      intentId: previous.id,                                  
      170 +      reference,                                              
      171 +      message: customerMessage('completed', await describe(ctx
          +.userId, input, reference)),                                  
      172 +    };                                                        
      173 +  }                                                           
      174 +  if (previous?.status === 'pending_verification') return rech
          +eck(ctx, previous, input);                                    
      175 +  if (!previous) {                                            
      176 +    // D8: an identical request while one is unverified is rec
          +onciled first, never sent blindly.                            
      177 +    const pending = db                                        
      178 +      .prepare(                                               
      179 +        "SELECT * FROM intents WHERE user_id=? AND payload=? A
          +ND status='pending_verification' ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 1",
      180 +      )                                                       
      181 +      .get(ctx.userId, payload) as Intent | undefined;        
      182 +    if (pending) {                                            
      183 +      const result = await recheck(ctx, pending, input, true);
      184 +      if (result.status !== 'failed') return result;          
      185 +    }                                                         
      186 +    db.prepare(                                               
      187 +      'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO intents(id,user_id,conversation_i
          +d,run_id,payload,status,bank_reference,created_at) VALUES(?,?,
          +?,?,?,?,?,?)',                                                
      188 +    ).run(                                                    
      189 +      ctx.intentId,                                           
      190 +      ctx.userId,                                             
      191 +      ctx.conversationId,                                     
      192 +      ctx.runId,                                              
      193 +      payload,                                                
      194 +      'created',                                              
      195 +      randomUUID(),                                           
      196 +      new Date().toISOString(),                               
      197 +    );                                                        
      198 +  }                                                           
      199 +  let intent = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent;              
      200 +  if (!intent.bank_reference) {                               
      201 +    db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET bank_reference=? WHERE id=?
          +').run(randomUUID(), intent.id);                              
      202 +    intent = byId.get(ctx.intentId) as Intent;                
      203 +  }                                                           
      204 +  try {                                                       
      205 +    const permission = await authorizeTransfer(ctx, input);   
      206 +    if (permission) return permission;                        
      207 +  } catch (e) {                                               
      208 +    // The bank could not be reached before sending: nothing l
          +eft the application.                                          
      209 +    if (e instanceof BankError && e.status >= 500)            
      210 +      return settle(ctx, intent, input, {                     
      211 +        state: 'not_executed',                                
      212 +        reason: 'not_sent',                                   
      213 +        error: e.message,                                     
      214 +      });                                                     
      215 +    throw e;                                                  
      216 +  }                                                           
      217 +  db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=? WHERE id=?').run('pr
          +ocessing', intent.id);                                        
      218 +  recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.started', { status: 'processing',
          + input, intentId: intent.id });                               
      219 +  let outcome: Outcome;                                       
      220 +  try {                                                       
      221 +    outcome = await dispatchTransfer(ctx.userId, input, intent
          +.bank_reference!, () =>                                       
      222 +      db                                                      
      223 +        .prepare('UPDATE intents SET last_sent_at=? WHERE id=?
          +')                                                            
      224 +        .run(new Date().toISOString(), intent.id),            
      225 +    );                                                        
      226 +  } catch {                                                   
      227 +    // Once sent, an unexpected error proves nothing about the
          + bank.                                                        
      228 +    outcome = { state: 'pending_verification', anomaly: false 
          +};                                                            
      229 +  }                                                           
      230 +  return settle(ctx, intent, input, outcome);                 
      231 +}

  Your two changes

  - Settle window: it is now measured from the last send. A new last_sent_at
    column is updated before every send, with a migration in src/db.ts that adds
    it to existing databases. Rows from before the change fall back to their
    creation time.
  - identical_completed wording: replaced with your text.

  One change you had not seen

  src/seed.ts now names its columns in the seeded-intent insert. Adding the new
  column would otherwise break npm run reset and the public seed test, because
  the old insert relied on the table having exactly ten columns.

  said "con cambios sin confirmar" although the code was committed, because the
  script counted its own evidence file as an uncommitted change. I fixed that
  check in ba8219d and re-recorded, so after-fix-1a.md now names a clean commit
  and includes R15. 7e1e39d remains in the history as a superseded record.

  R15's result, in the customer's words: the first request answered
  pending_verification; the identical second request answered "We have checked
  with the bank: your earlier transfer of €10.00 to Bruno Vidal (Horizon
  account) was completed, once. … We have not sent it again." There was one
  charge. As the doc note says, the simulator can only reproduce the branch
  where the lookup resolves the pending transfer; the "still pending" branch has
  no reproduction.

  A gap I found while preparing your checklist

  D8 only protects while a transfer is pending. Once the lookup has resolved it
  as completed, a customer who types "try again" in the chat starts a new
  message. The model does not know the earlier payment completed, so it can send
  a second one. Fix 2's confirmation is the hard guarantee. For part (b) I
  propose a cheaper first barrier: give the agent the verified status of the
  transfers already requested in the conversation.

  // What an older build left without a verified outcome.
  const LEGACY_TRANSPORT_ERROR = 'No response received from the bank.';
  // Reconciliation on read: asks the bank about this customer's unverified 
  intents.
  // Never sends money.
  export async function reconcileIntents(userId: string, reference?: string) {
    const rows = appDb()
      .prepare(
        `SELECT * FROM intents WHERE user_id=?1 AND bank_reference IS NOT NULL
           AND (?2 IS NULL OR bank_reference=?2)
           AND (status='pending_verification'
             OR (status='processing' AND COALESCE(last_sent_at,created_at)<?3)
             OR (status='failed' AND operation_id IS NULL AND error=?4))
         ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 5`,
      )
      .all(
        userId,
        reference ?? null,
        new Date(Date.now() - SETTLE_MS).toISOString(),
        LEGACY_TRANSPORT_ERROR,
      ) as Intent[];
    const results: ActionResult[] = [];
    for (const intent of rows) {
      const ctx = {
        userId,
        conversationId: intent.conversation_id,
        runId: randomUUID(),
        intentId: intent.id,
      };
      try {
        results.push(await recheck(ctx, intent, JSON.parse(intent.payload)));
      } catch {}
    }
    return results.map(({ intentId, status, message, reference }) => ({
      intentId,
      status,
      message,
      reference,
    }));
  }
  // Verified status of the transfers already requested in a conversation, for 
  the agent's context.
  export function conversationTransfers(userId: string, conversationId: string)
  {
    const label: Record<string, string> = {
      completed: 'completed, confirmed by the bank',
      failed: 'not executed, no money moved',
      pending_verification: 'pending verification, outcome unknown',
    };
    const rows = appDb()
      .prepare(
        'SELECT * FROM intents WHERE user_id=? AND conversation_id=? ORDER BY 
  created_at DESC LIMIT 5',
      )
      .all(userId, conversationId) as Intent[];
    return rows.map((intent) => {
      const input = JSON.parse(intent.payload) as TransferInput;
      return `- ${label[intent.status] || 'in progress'}:
  ${money(input.amountCents)} from ${input.fromAccountId} to
  ${input.toAccountId}, description ${JSON.stringify(input.concept)}, reference
  ${intent.bank_reference}`;
    });
  }

  Order of work I propose for (b)

  1. Add R16 to the script, commit it, and run it alone so it is recorded red
     before the code exists.
  2. Write the code above, then Prettier, tsc, npm test.

  What to know about this diff

  - The conversation list in the agent's context is new scope. It was not in the
    approved design for (b); it is my answer to the "try again" gap. It reduces
    the risk but depends on the model.
  - The panel reuses the existing approvals style. I have not seen it rendered;
    your browser test is the first real look.
  - One extra bank lookup happens on each operation_status call, and
    reconciliation checks at most five intents per page load.
  - R8 will stay green in later runs for a trivial reason. The app database is
    not reset between runs, so once the historic intent is reconciled it stays
    completed until npm run reset.

  # Fix 1. Prueba de aceptación humana

  La hace Fernando en el navegador, con un escenario de fallo activo. Cada
  escenario solo afecta a la primera operación nueva: hay que volver a ejecutar
  el comando antes de cada prueba.

  Preparación: `nvm use`, servicios en marcha con `npm run dev`, y la aplicación
  abierta como Lucía.

  ## A. Formulario, respuesta perdida

  1. `npm run scenario -- lost-response`
  2. Transfers → Aurora account → Bruno Vidal → 10 → «Aceptación A» → Send
  transfer.

  | Debo ver | Sí / No |
  |---|---|
  | Aviso: «Your transfer of €10.00 to Bruno Vidal (Horizon account) is
  complete. The bank has confirmed it. Reference: …» | |
  | En Overview, un solo movimiento «Transfer · Aceptación A» de −€10.00 | |
  | Como Bruno, un solo ingreso de +€10.00 | |

  ## B. Formulario, el banco no deja consultar

  1. `npm run scenario -- read-unavailable`
  2. Misma transferencia con «Aceptación B».

  | Debo ver | Sí / No |
  |---|---|
  | Aviso que dice que el banco aún no la ha confirmado, «Please do not send it
  again: you could pay twice» y una referencia | |
  | En ningún sitio aparece «failed» | |
  | Panel «Transfers checked with the bank»: «We have checked with the bank: …
  was completed, once.» | |
  | Al pulsar Send transfer otra vez sin cambiar nada: mensaje de completada con
  la misma referencia | |
  | En Overview, un solo movimiento «Transfer · Aceptación B» | |

  El aviso de pendiente y el panel aparecen casi a la vez: el simulador solo
  falla una consulta, y la aplicación vuelve a preguntar al refrescar las
  cuentas.

  ## C. Chat, respuesta perdida

  1. `npm run scenario -- lost-response`
  2. Conversación nueva: «Send EUR 10 from my Aurora account to Bruno.
  Description: Aceptación C».

  | Debo ver | Sí / No |
  |---|---|
  | El asistente dice que está completada y da la referencia | |
  | Un solo movimiento «Transfer · Aceptación C» | |

  ## D. Chat, el banco no deja consultar

  1. `npm run scenario -- read-unavailable`
  2. Conversación nueva: «Send EUR 10 from my Aurora account to Bruno.
  Description: Aceptación D».
  3. Después, en la misma conversación: «Try again».

  | Debo ver | Sí / No |
  |---|---|
  | Tras el paso 2: el asistente dice que el banco no la ha confirmado, que no
  la repita, y da la referencia; no dice que falló | |
  | Panel «Transfers checked with the bank» con «completed, once» | |
  | Tras el paso 3: el asistente dice que ya se completó y pregunta si quiero un
  segundo pago distinto | |
  | Un solo movimiento «Transfer · Aceptación D» | |

  Límites conocidos: el texto del chat lo redacta el modelo y puede variar. El
  paso 3 depende del modelo; la garantía firme llega con Fix 2. En C y D el
  dinero aún sale sin confirmación previa, que es justo lo que corrige Fix 2.

  draft.

  What the run showed

  9 of 13 cases pass. The two that should have turned green did not:

  ┌──────┬──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Case │    Expected after (b)    │                Actual                 │
  ├──────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ R8   │ app intent completed     │ still failed · «No response received  │
  │      │                          │ from the bank.»                       │
  ├──────┼──────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ R16  │ completed on opening the │ "ninguna noticia de la transferencia" │
  │      │  accounts                │                                       │
  └──────┴──────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────┘

  Everything else is unchanged from after-fix-1a (R5 and R10 red as expected,
  the rest green), and tsc and the 18 tests pass.

  Root cause

  The reconciliation query never runs: it throws on every call. I wrote it with
  numbered placeholders (?1 … ?4) and passed the values positionally, which the
  SQLite driver rejects. I confirmed it read-only against the app database:

  - Positional values, as in my code: RangeError: Too many parameter values were
    provided.
  - Same query with the values passed by number: returns the three rows it
    should (the historic Lucía intent and two pending ones left by earlier
    script runs).

  The error was invisible because every caller wraps reconciliation in .catch(()
  => []), so the accounts page never breaks. That safety net hid my bug.
  Neither tsc nor the tests could catch it, since no test executes that query.

  Proposed fix (not applied)

  1. The query: pass the four values by number, which is what the placeholders
     expect.

  -    .all(
  -      userId,
  -      reference ?? null,
  -      new Date(Date.now() - SETTLE_MS).toISOString(),
  -      LEGACY_TRANSPORT_ERROR,
  -    ) as Intent[];
  +    .all({
  +      1: userId,
  +      2: reference ?? null,
  +      3: new Date(Date.now() - SETTLE_MS).toISOString(),
  +      4: LEGACY_TRANSPORT_ERROR,
  +    }) as Intent[];

  2. Stop swallowing the error silently. The three callers keep protecting the
     customer, but log the failure the way the app already does elsewhere
     (console.error('reconcile_failed', error name)), so a broken check shows up
     in the terminal.
  3. A test that would have caught it. One test that runs reconcileIntents
     against an empty temporary database and expects an empty list. It needs no
     bank, and it fails today.

  One more thing you should know: submission/03-fixes/evidence/after-fix-1b.md
  exists on disk with these failing results. It is uncommitted and will be
  overwritten by the next run.

  Still owed from your last message and not yet written: D9 and the acceptance
  checklist with the screenshot steps. I held them back when the run failed; I
  will write them once this is resolved.

  Do you approve the three changes above?

  Part (c) diff: evidence per transfer step

  What gets stored. Only transfer steps change. Each one keeps an allow-list of
  fields: intent, reference, which step it was, the bank's answer status, the
  verified outcome and its reason, and the operation id. The timestamp is the
  event's existing created_at. Tool events stay exactly as they are today, and
  there are no UI changes.

  src/telemetry.ts

  +// Transfer steps keep what an operator needs to reconstruct a case, and 
  nothing else:
  +// identifiers, the bank's answer status and the verified outcome. Never 
  payloads or secrets.
  +const TRANSFER_FIELDS = [
  +  'intentId',
  +  'reference',
  +  'step',
  +  'bankStatus',
  +  'status',
  +  'reason',
  +  'operationId',
  +] as const;
   export function recordEvent(ctx: ToolContext, kind: string, data:
  Record<string, unknown>) {
  +  const kept = kind.startsWith('transfer.')
  +    ? Object.fromEntries(
  +        TRANSFER_FIELDS.filter((k) => data[k] !== undefined).map((k) => [k, 
  data[k]]),
  +      )
  +    : { tool: data.tool, status: data.status };
     appDb()
       .prepare('INSERT INTO events VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)')
       .run(
         randomUUID(),
         ctx.runId,
         ctx.userId,
         ctx.conversationId,
         kind,
  -      JSON.stringify({ tool: data.tool, status: data.status }),
  +      JSON.stringify(kept),
         new Date().toISOString(),
       );
   }

   ...

  +    throw new BankError(504, 'No response received from the bank.', true);

  Evidence for this part: a new case R17

  I propose the same order as part (b): add the case, record it red, then write
  the code.

  - Story: "Lucía envía 10 EUR a Bruno y se pierde la respuesta del banco: si
    mañana pregunta, ¿queda constancia de cada paso?"
  - Check: the app's events for that intent show the send with its bank status,
    the lookup with its bank status, and the verified outcome with the reference
    and operation id.
  - How: the script reads the events table read-only, as D7 allows. This needs a
    small helper in the script to read several rows.

  R10 stays "NO CUMPLE" (the operator view is still out of scope); only its
  informative line will show the richer fields.

  What to know

  - Amounts and accounts are not duplicated into events. They live in the intent
    row, which each event points to by intent id.
  - The bank's error text is no longer stored in the event, only a reason code;
    the text is still on the intent row.
  - "No answer" is stored as an empty bank status, so an operator can tell a
    timeout from a real 504 from the bank.
  - Older events keep their old shape (status only); nothing is migrated.

  One slip: my first version of R17 had a type error and failed tsc; nothing was
  committed until I fixed it.

  How to prepare your acceptance test

  1. Decide whether to reset (I recommend it). The app database holds over a
  hundred script-generated transfers for Lucía. A reset gives you the delivered
  starting state, but it deletes your local conversations; your screenshots are
  already safe in git.

  # in the terminal running the services: Ctrl+C, then
  nvm use
  npm run reset
  npm run dev

  If you prefer not to reset, at least restart npm run dev, so the server starts
  on the final code.

  2. Create the screenshot folder.

  mkdir -p submission/notes/evidencia/aceptacion

  3. Open http://127.0.0.1:3000 and select Lucía. If you did reset, you should
  see something before any test: a panel "Transfers checked with the bank"
  saying her 85 EUR dinner payment to Bruno "was completed, once". That is the
  historic case (R8) being reconciled live when she opens her accounts. It is
  not in the checklist; I suggest a screenshot named 0-historico-lucia.png.

  4. Run each section of submission/03-fixes/01-acceptance-checklist.md in 
  order. Use a second terminal for the scenario commands, with nvm use first.

  ┌─────────────────┬──────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┐
  │     Section     │   Command to run first   │            User            │
  ├─────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ A. Form, lost   │ npm run scenario --      │ Lucía, then Bruno for A-3, │
  │ response        │ lost-response            │  then back to Lucía        │
  ├─────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ B. Form, bank   │ npm run scenario --      │ Lucía                      │
  │ lookup down     │ read-unavailable         │                            │
  ├─────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ C. Chat, lost   │ npm run scenario --      │ Lucía, new conversation    │
  │ response        │ lost-response            │                            │
  ├─────────────────┼──────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ D. Chat, bank   │ npm run scenario --      │ Lucía, new conversation    │
  │ lookup down     │ read-unavailable         │                            │
  └─────────────────┴──────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────┘

  5. Things to keep in mind while testing:

  - Run the scenario command immediately before each section. Each scenario only
    affects the first new transfer after the command.
  - Do not run npm run repro between sections. It resets the bank and your test
    movements disappear.
  - C and D need your OpenAI key, and each chat turn can take tens of seconds.
  - Watch the services terminal. Lines starting with reconcile_failed,
    reconcile_intent_failed, transfer_recheck_failed or transfer_dispatch_failed
    mean a check failed silently for the customer; there should be none. Tell
    me if one appears.
  - In C and D the money still leaves without a confirmation step. That is
    expected until Fix 2.

  One thing still open on my side

  01-transfer-integrity.md still says "diseño aprobado" and its table of
  expected results does not list R16 and R17. I propose updating its status and
  adding a short before/after summary pointing to the evidence files, once your
  acceptance result is in so that it can be recorded there too. I will show you
  that diff first.

  Proposed fix for O2 and O3 (not written)

  O2: the notice and the panel contradict each other. The cause is that a
  pending result is shown in two places: the yellow notice, which never updates,
  and the panel, which does. The smallest fix is to give each transfer a single
  place:

  - A pending_verification result from the form goes into the "Transfers checked
    with the bank" panel instead of the yellow notice.
  - The panel already replaces an entry when the same transfer is resolved, so
    the pending text turns into "completed, once" in place.

  That is about five lines in submitTransfer in app/page.tsx, with no server
  change.

  O3: the notice follows the customer across pages. The smallest fix is to clear
  the yellow notice whenever the customer changes tab. That is one line in
  app/page.tsx.

  Where they belong: both in Fix 1, as one small UI commit.

  - O2 is about Fix 1's own states: it is the "pending" language contradicting
    itself on screen.
  - O3 was already there before our changes, but Fix 1 made it visible by
    putting long messages with references in that notice.
  - Fix 2 will rework the same notice for the confirmation step, and I would
    rather start it from a clean base.

  O1 stays with Fix 2.

  How it would be verified. No script covers the UI, so the evidence would be
  you repeating section B (one screenshot showing no contradiction) plus one tab
  change.

  Side effect to accept. With the O2 change, the pending text will be visible
  only for an instant in the simulator, because the next refresh resolves it. It
  stays on screen whenever the bank still cannot answer.

  Proposed commit

  "docs: record fix 1 human acceptance results", including the checklist and the
  16 screenshots.

  O2/O3 diff.

  app/page.tsx: O3, the notice does not follow the customer to another page

  One change beyond the UI, which you can veto. While writing O2 I found a hole
  it would open. The panel's pending entry is refreshed from what the server
  reports. If the server's check itself breaks, the server reports an empty
  list, and the panel would drop the pending text without telling the customer.
  To avoid that, the server would say "the check failed" instead of "nothing to
  report", and the page would keep what it shows:

   // src/banking/actions.ts
   export const reconcileSafely = (userId: string, reference?: string) =>
     reconcileIntents(userId, reference).catch((e) => {
       logFailure('reconcile_failed', e);

  Draft of submission/03-fixes/02-review-before-execution.md

  # Fix 2. Revisión explícita antes de ejecutar

  - **Estado:** diseño pendiente de aprobación. Aún no hay código.
  - **Hallazgos que corrige:** H4 y H5 de `01-diagnosis.md` (regla 3 del
  contrato). Cubre H14 (cuenta de origen ambigua) y la observación humana O1.
  - **Evidencia «antes»:** R5 no cumple en `evidence/after-fix-1-ui.md`: el
  dinero sale sin confirmación.
  - **Principio:** el modelo propone, la persona decide.

  ## 1. El principio y la confirmación por chat

  El modelo nunca autoriza dinero. Puede preparar una propuesta; solo la ejecuta
  una acción explícita del cliente en la interfaz: un botón sobre un resumen
  que muestra importe, cuenta de origen con su nombre, destinatario con nombre y
  cuenta, y descripción.

  Esto se garantiza por construcción, no por instrucciones: la herramienta
  `transfer_money` del agente deja de enviar dinero y pasa a crear propuestas, y
  el agente no tiene ninguna herramienta para confirmar.

  **¿Aceptar también un «sí» escrito en el chat? Recomendación: no.**

  | A favor | Riesgo |
  |---|---|
  | Continuidad: el cliente no sale de la conversación. | El modelo tendría que
  interpretar un «sí», «vale» u «ok» que puede responder a otra pregunta. |
  | Canales sin botones (voz, mensajería). | El contrato lo excluye: «An
  informational conversation alone does not authorize payment»
  (`docs/contracts.md:7`). |
  | | El texto de un documento o de la descripción de una transferencia podría
  simular un consentimiento. |
  | | Un «sí» no está ligado a unos datos exactos: entre la propuesta y la
  ejecución el modelo podría cambiar importe o destino. |
  | | No tiene caducidad ni uso único. |

  Si el cliente escribe «sí, confírmalo», el asistente le indica que pulse el
  botón de la tarjeta de revisión. Un canal sin interfaz necesitaría su propio
  mecanismo de confirmación ligado a la propuesta; queda fuera de alcance.

  ## 2. Estados de una propuesta

  ```mermaid
  stateDiagram-v2
      [*] --> proposed: El cliente o el agente pide una transferencia
      proposed --> proposed: Misma intención otra vez, se devuelve la misma
  propuesta
      proposed --> confirmed: El titular pulsa Confirm transfer antes de que
  caduque
      proposed --> expired: Pasan 10 minutos sin confirmar
      proposed --> cancelled: El titular pulsa Cancel
      confirmed --> intent_sent: Se envía con la referencia estable de la
  intención
      confirmed --> confirmed: Confirmar otra vez devuelve el resultado de la
  misma intención
      intent_sent --> completed: Hecho verificado en el banco
      intent_sent --> not_executed: Rechazo verificado en el banco
      intent_sent --> pending_verification: Incertidumbre
      expired --> [*]
      cancelled --> [*]
      completed --> [*]
      not_executed --> [*]
  ```

  A partir de `confirmed` manda la máquina de estados de Fix 1
  (`01-transfer-integrity.md`): una referencia por intención, consulta antes de
  afirmar y «pendiente de verificar» ante la duda.

  **Confirmar dos veces produce un solo pago**, por dos barreras:

  - **Uso único.** La confirmación se consume en una sola operación atómica de
  base de datos. De dos clics simultáneos solo uno la consume.
  - **Referencia estable.** El clic que no la consume trata la propuesta como ya
  confirmada y devuelve el resultado de esa misma intención. Si el primer envío
  aún está en curso, cualquier envío usa la misma referencia, y el banco lo
  trata como el mismo pago.

  Una propuesta caducada o cancelada no se reactiva: hay que pedir la
  transferencia de nuevo.

  ## 3. Lo que comprueba la confirmación (H5)

  `POST /api/approvals/:id/confirm` ejecuta únicamente lo que está guardado en
  la propuesta. El cuerpo de la petición no se lee.

  | Comprobación | Si falla |
  |---|---|
  | La propuesta existe y es del usuario de la sesión. | 404 «Proposal not
  found.», igual que hoy. No revela si existe para otra persona. |
  | La intención asociada es del mismo usuario y sus datos son idénticos a los
  de la propuesta. | 409. No se envía nada. |
  | No está cancelada. | Resultado `cancelled`. No se envía nada. |
  | No ha caducado. | Resultado `expired`. No se envía nada. |
  | No se ha usado ya. | Se devuelve el resultado de la intención ya confirmada.
  No se envía de nuevo. |
  | La cuenta de origen sigue siendo del titular en el momento de ejecutar. |
  Resultado «no ejecutada». |

  **Caducidad propuesta: 10 minutos.** Da tiempo a leer y decidir dentro de una
  conversación, y es lo bastante corta para que el saldo y el contexto no hayan
  cambiado y para que una pantalla desatendida no sea un riesgo. La tarjeta
  muestra la hora de caducidad.

  Cambios de datos necesarios: ninguno en el esquema. La tabla `approvals` ya
  tiene `payload`, `expires_at` y `consumed_at`. La intención gana dos estados,
  `proposed` y `cancelled`.

  ## 4. Cómo se aplica en cada entrada

  | Entrada | Antes | Diseño |
  |---|---|---|
  | Herramienta del chat | `transfer_money` envía el dinero. | `transfer_money`
  crea una propuesta y devuelve `requires_confirmation`. El asistente explica
  que no se ha enviado nada y que hay que confirmar en la tarjeta. La tarjeta
  aparece bajo el chat. |
  | Formulario | «Send transfer» envía el dinero. | El botón pasa a ser «Review
  transfer» y crea la propuesta. La tarjeta de revisión tiene «Confirm transfer»
  y «Cancel». Tras confirmar, el formulario se vacía (O1). |
  | API | `POST /api/actions` devuelve `completed`. | `POST /api/actions`
  devuelve `requires_confirmation` con la propuesta (`approval.id`,
  `approval.expiresAt`, datos y resumen). `POST /api/approvals/:id/confirm`
  ejecuta. |

  **Compatibilidad con `docs/contracts.md`.** El diseño usa la superficie ya
  documentada, sin cambiarla: `POST /api/actions`, `POST
  /api/approvals/:id/confirm` como «confirmation entry point for proposals»,
  `dashboard.approvals` para mostrarlas, y resultados con `status` y los campos
  `operation`, `approval` o `error`. Se añade una sola ruta, `POST
  /api/approvals/:id/cancel`.

  **La misma intención otra vez.** Repetir una petición con el mismo `intentId`
  devuelve la misma propuesta mientras siga viva; si ya se confirmó, devuelve el
  resultado verificado de Fix 1. Una intención «no ejecutada» que se repite
  genera una propuesta nueva: ningún envío ocurre sin una confirmación vigente.

  **El resultado vuelve a la conversación.** Cuando el cliente confirma una
  propuesta nacida en el chat, el mensaje de resultado se añade a esa
  conversación. Sin esto, el chat se quedaría en «por favor, confirma» y ni el
  cliente ni un operador verían cómo terminó.

  **Cuenta de origen (H14).** La tarjeta muestra siempre la cuenta de origen con
  su nombre. Además, las instrucciones del agente le piden preguntar cuando el
  cliente tiene varias cuentas y no ha dicho cuál; esa parte depende del modelo,
  la tarjeta no.

  ## 5. Cómo encaja D8

  Con la confirmación explícita, una petición idéntica deja de retenerse: se
  convierte en una propuesta con una advertencia, y solo la confirmación del
  cliente envía un segundo pago.

  La advertencia aparece en la tarjeta cuando el cliente tiene otra
  transferencia con los mismos datos que:

  - está pendiente de verificar (antes se concilia con el banco, como en D8), o
  - se completó en las últimas 24 horas.

  La advertencia se calcula en el momento de mostrar la tarjeta, no al crearla,
  así que refleja el estado real cuando el cliente decide. Esto cubre también
  O1: un segundo clic en el formulario tras un pago completado muestra la
  advertencia en lugar de pagar otra vez.

  Este punto modifica D8 y se registrará como D11.

  ## 6. Lo que lee el cliente

  **Tarjeta de revisión**

  | Elemento | Inglés | Traducción |
  |---|---|---|
  | Título | Review your transfer | Revisa tu transferencia |
  | Importe | Amount: {amount} | Importe: {importe} |
  | Origen | From: {account label} ({masked IBAN}) | Desde: {nombre de la
  cuenta} ({IBAN enmascarado}) |
  | Destino | To: {recipient name} · {recipient account label} ({masked IBAN}) |
  Para: {nombre del destinatario} · {nombre de su cuenta} ({IBAN enmascarado})
  |
  | Descripción | Description: {concept} | Descripción: {concepto} |
  | Pie | Nothing is sent until you confirm. This proposal expires at {time}. |
  No se envía nada hasta que confirmes. Esta propuesta caduca a las {hora}. |
  | Botones | Confirm transfer · Cancel | Confirmar transferencia · Cancelar |
  | Advertencia (pendiente) | You already have an identical transfer being
  verified (reference {reference}). If you confirm, this will be a second,
  separate payment. | Ya tienes una transferencia idéntica en verificación
  (referencia {referencia}). Si confirmas, será un segundo pago distinto. |
  | Advertencia (completada) | You already completed an identical transfer at
  {time} (reference {reference}). If you confirm, this will be a second,
  separate payment. | Ya completaste una transferencia idéntica a las {hora}
  (referencia {referencia}). Si confirmas, será un segundo pago distinto. |

  **Resultados**

  | Situación | Inglés | Traducción |
  |---|---|---|
  | Propuesta creada (chat y formulario) | We have prepared a transfer of
  {amount} from {account} to {recipient}. Nothing has been sent yet. Please
  review it and press Confirm transfer. This proposal expires at {time}. | Hemos
  preparado una transferencia de {importe} desde {cuenta} a {destinatario}.
  Todavía no se ha enviado nada. Revísala y pulsa Confirmar transferencia. Esta
  propuesta caduca a las {hora}. |
  | El cliente escribe «sí» en el chat | We cannot confirm a payment from the
  chat. Please press Confirm transfer on the review card. | No podemos confirmar
  un pago desde el chat. Pulsa Confirmar transferencia en la tarjeta de
  revisión. |
  | Caducada | This proposal has expired and nothing was sent. If you still want
  to make the transfer, start it again. | Esta propuesta ha caducado y no se ha
  enviado nada. Si aún quieres hacer la transferencia, iníciala de nuevo. |
  | Cancelada | You cancelled this transfer. Nothing was sent. | Has cancelado
  esta transferencia. No se ha enviado nada. |
  | Confirmada | Los mensajes de Fix 1: completada, no ejecutada o pendiente de
  verificar. | — |
  | Confirmada por segunda vez | El mismo mensaje de resultado que la primera
  vez. | — |

  La frase del «sí» en el chat la redacta el modelo a partir de sus
  instrucciones; su texto exacto no se puede garantizar.

  ## 7. Casos de reproducción

  | Caso | Historia del cliente | Pasa cuando | ¿Rojo antes del código? |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | R5 (existente) | Lucía pide una transferencia: ¿puede revisar antes de que
  salga el dinero? | Sin confirmación no se mueve dinero y la propuesta es
  visible. | Ya está en rojo. |
  | R18 | Lucía pulsa confirmar dos veces seguidas: ¿paga una sola vez? | Dos
  confirmaciones simultáneas y una tercera después: un solo cargo y la misma
  operación. | Sí |
  | R20 | Bruno intenta confirmar la propuesta de Lucía: ¿se le impide? | 404 y
  ningún cargo; Lucía aún puede confirmarla. | Sí |
  | R21 | Alguien altera la confirmación para cambiar importe y destino: ¿se
  ejecuta lo que Lucía revisó? | Un cargo de 10,00 EUR a Bruno y nada a nadie
  más. | Sí |
  | R22 | Lucía cancela la propuesta y después llega una confirmación: ¿se envía
  algo? | Ningún cargo. | Sí |
  | R15 (se reescribe) | Con un pago pendiente de verificar, Lucía pide otro
  idéntico: ¿se le avisa antes de pagar dos veces? | La segunda petición no
  mueve dinero por sí sola y su propuesta lleva la advertencia. | Sí, con la
  comprobación nueva |

  **Propuesta caducada: prueba en `tests/`, no en el script.** Esperar 10
  minutos reales no es viable, y el script solo puede leer la base de datos
  (D7), así que no puede adelantar la caducidad. Una prueba con base de datos
  temporal crea una propuesta ya caducada y comprueba que confirmarla no envía
  nada.

  Dos límites de esta evidencia:

  - **R18, R20, R21 y R22 fallarán antes del código por el mismo motivo que 
  R5:** hoy no existe ninguna propuesta que confirmar. Registrarlos en rojo
  documenta el orden, pero su valor real es como vigilancia después.
  - **H5 no se puede reproducir antes del código.** El punto de confirmación
  actual es inalcanzable porque nada crea propuestas; su evidencia es la lectura
  del código (`app/api/[...path]/route.ts:135-156`).

  ## 8. Riesgos de regresión (segundo orden)

  **Cómo siguen funcionando R1-R17.** El script ya confirma por el cliente: su
  función de envío sigue un `requires_confirmation` llamando a `POST
  /api/approvals/:id/confirm`. Cada caso pasa a ser «pedir y confirmar», y todo
  lo de Fix 1 ocurre después de la confirmación.

  | Caso | Qué cambia | Riesgo |
  |---|---|---|
  | R1, R3, R7, R14, R17 | Pedir, confirmar y verificar. | Bajo. |
  | R2 | El segundo y el tercer pago idénticos llevan advertencia, pero se
  confirman. | Bajo: deben seguir siendo tres cargos. |
  | R4 | La segunda petición con el mismo `intentId` devuelve el resultado
  completado, no una propuesta nueva. | Medio: es la interacción más delicada
  entre Fix 1 y Fix 2. |
  | R13, R16 | La primera confirmación queda pendiente; lo demás, igual. |
  Medio. |
  | R15 | Cambia su comprobación (sección 7). | Es un cambio de criterio, no una
  regresión. |
  | R8, R10 | Sin cambios. | — |

  **Otros riesgos**

  | Riesgo | Mitigación |
  |---|---|
  | Un evaluador externo que espere `completed` directamente de `POST
  /api/actions` recibirá `requires_confirmation`. | Es lo que exige la regla 3 y
  lo que la interfaz original ya contemplaba. La superficie de confirmación es
  la documentada. Es el mayor cambio visible desde fuera y se asume. |
  | El modelo dice «hecho» cuando solo ha propuesto. | El resultado de la
  herramienta dice que no se ha enviado nada, y la lista de transferencias de la
  conversación (D9) incluirá «propuesta, sin enviar». Depende del modelo; se
  comprueba en la prueba humana. |
  | Propuestas antiguas acumuladas en la tarjeta tras editar el formulario. |
  Cada tarjeta muestra sus datos y tiene «Cancel»; caducan solas a los 10
  minutos. |
  | La conciliación de Fix 1 trata una propuesta sin confirmar como una
  transferencia sin verificar. | Una propuesta nunca se ha enviado: la
  conciliación solo mira intenciones enviadas. Se añade una prueba. |
  | Una intención «no ejecutada» repetida ya no se reenvía sola. | Cambio
  deliberado respecto a Fix 1: ahora genera una propuesta nueva. |
  | La advertencia de pago idéntico molesta en pagos repetidos legítimos. | Es
  solo un aviso; no bloquea. |

  **Prueba de aceptación humana.** Tras Fix 2 se repiten las secciones C y D del
  chat y se añaden: el flujo del formulario con revisión (O1), el «sí» escrito
  en el chat, y el caso de Elena de los datos iniciales («Before deciding
  whether to send EUR 25 to Hugo, what would I need to review?»), que no debe
  mover dinero.

  Decisions I need from you

  1. Chat-text confirmation. I recommend no, for the reasons in section 1. The
     guarantee is structural: the agent simply has no way to confirm.
  2. Expiry: 10 minutes. Shorter is safer but more annoying mid-conversation;
     say if you prefer 5.
  3. D8 changes. With your point 5, an identical request is no longer withheld;
     it becomes a proposal with a warning. I extended the warning to identical
     transfers completed in the last 24 hours, which is what closes O1. This
     would be D11.
  4. R15's check changes. Under the new behaviour, the script's auto-confirm
     would send a second payment and R15 would fail its current check. I propose
     rewriting what R15 asserts (no money moves without confirmation, and the
     warning is present). This is changing a test's criterion, so I want your
     explicit approval.
  5. Additions you did not ask for:
     - a Cancel button and route;
     - the outcome message written back into the conversation;
     - clearing the form after a confirmed transfer.
       Each can be dropped.
  6. Expired proposals are tested in tests/, not in the script, because the
     script may only read the database. The alternative is a narrow exception to
     D7.
  7. Wording in section 6 is for your approval.

  Waiting for your OK or changes before writing the file.

  export const proposalMessage = (d: Details, minutes: number) =>
    `We have prepared a transfer of ${d.amount} from ${d.account} to
  ${d.recipient}. Nothing has been sent yet. Please review it and press Confirm
  transfer. This proposal expires in ${minutes} minute${minutes === 1 ? '' : 
  's'}.`;
  export const EXPIRED_MESSAGE =
    'This proposal has expired and nothing was sent. If you still want to make 
  the transfer, start it again.';
  export const CANCELLED_MESSAGE = 'You cancelled this transfer. Nothing was 
  sent.';
  export const warningMessage = (kind: 'pending' | 'completed', reference:
  string) =>
    kind === 'pending'
      ? `You already have an identical transfer being verified (reference 
  ${reference}). If you confirm, this will be a second, separate payment.`
      : `You already completed an identical transfer in the last 24 hours 
  (reference ${reference}). If you confirm, this will be a second, separate
  payment.`;

  src/banking/actions.ts

  Asking for a transfer now only creates a proposal. transferMoney keeps its

    // Same intent again: a verified or in-flight one reports its state and 
  never proposes anew.
    if (previous?.status === 'completed' && previous.operation_id)
      return completedResult(ctx, previous, input);
    if (previous && ['pending_verification', 'confirmed',
  'processing'].includes(previous.status))
      return recheck(ctx, previous, input);

  warning is accurate.

  New: the proposal and its warning

  const APPROVAL_TTL_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
  const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  type Approval = {
    id: string;
    user_id: string;
    intent_id: string;
    payload: string;
    expires_at: string;
    consumed_at: string | null;
  };
  // An identical transfer of this customer that is unverified or was completed 
  recently (D11).
  // Computed when the proposal is shown, so it reflects the state at decision 
  time.
  export function identicalWarning(userId: string, payload: string, intentId:
  string) {
    const other = appDb()
      .prepare(
        `SELECT status,bank_reference,COALESCE(last_sent_at,created_at) AS at 
  FROM intents
         WHERE user_id=? AND payload=? AND id<>?
           AND (status='pending_verification'
             OR (status='completed' AND COALESCE(last_sent_at,created_at)>?))
         ORDER BY status='pending_verification' DESC, at DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get(userId, payload, intentId, new Date(Date.now() -
  DAY_MS).toISOString()) as
      { status: string; bank_reference: string; at: string } | undefined;
    if (!other) return null;
    const kind = other.status === 'completed' ? ('completed' as const) :
  ('pending' as const);
    return {
      kind,
      reference: other.bank_reference,
      at: other.at,
      text: warningMessage(kind, other.bank_reference),
    };
  }
  async function propose(ctx: ToolContext, intent: Intent, input: TransferInput)
  {
    const db = appDb(),
      now = Date.now();
    // One live proposal per intent: asking again returns the same one.
    let approval = db
      .prepare(
        'SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE intent_id=? AND consumed_at IS NULL AND 
  expires_at>? ORDER BY expires_at DESC LIMIT 1',
      )
      .get(intent.id, new Date(now).toISOString()) as Approval | undefined;
    if (!approval) {
      const created: Approval = {
        id: randomUUID(),
        user_id: ctx.userId,
        intent_id: intent.id,
        payload: intent.payload,
        expires_at: new Date(now + APPROVAL_TTL_MS).toISOString(),
        consumed_at: null,
      };
      db.transaction(() => {
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO approvals VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(
          created.id, created.user_id, created.intent_id, created.payload,
  created.expires_at, null,
        );
        db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=?,error=NULL WHERE 
  id=?').run('proposed', intent.id);
      })();
      approval = created;
      recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.proposed', {
        status: 'proposed',
        intentId: intent.id,
        reference: intent.bank_reference,
        approvalId: created.id,
      });
    }
    const details = await describe(ctx.userId, input, intent.bank_reference!);
    const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((Date.parse(approval.expires_at) -
  now) / 60000));
    return {
      status: 'requires_confirmation',
      intentId: intent.id,
      approval: {
        id: approval.id,
        expiresAt: approval.expires_at,
        payload: input,
        summary: { amount: details.amount, account: details.account, recipient:
  details.recipient, concept: input.concept },
        warning: identicalWarning(ctx.userId, intent.payload, intent.id),
      },
      message: proposalMessage(details, minutes),
    };
  }

  New: confirmation (H5)

  export async function confirmTransfer(userId: string, approvalId: string):
  Promise<ActionResult> {
    if (person(userId)?.role !== 'customer')
      throw new HttpError(403, 'A customer account is required.');
    const db = appDb();
    const approval = db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE id=? AND user_id=?')
      .get(approvalId, userId) as Approval | undefined;
    if (!approval) throw new HttpError(404, 'Proposal not found.');
    const byId = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM intents WHERE id=? AND user_id=?');
    let intent = byId.get(approval.intent_id, userId) as Intent | undefined;
    if (!intent) throw new HttpError(404, 'Intent not found.');
    // What is executed is exactly what was reviewed: the stored proposal, never
  the request.
    if (intent.payload !== approval.payload)
      throw new HttpError(409, 'This proposal no longer matches its transfer.');
    const input = transferSchema.parse(JSON.parse(approval.payload));
    const ctx = {
      userId,
      conversationId: intent.conversation_id,
      runId: randomUUID(),
      intentId: intent.id,
      approvalId: approval.id,
    };
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    // Single use: of two simultaneous confirmations only one consumes the 
  proposal.
    const won = db.transaction(() => {
      const used =
        db
          .prepare('UPDATE approvals SET consumed_at=? WHERE id=? AND 
  consumed_at IS NULL AND expires_at>?')
          .run(now, approval.id, now).changes === 1;
      if (used) db.prepare('UPDATE intents SET status=? WHERE 
  id=?').run('confirmed', approval.intent_id);
      return used;
    })();
    if (won) {
      recordEvent(ctx, 'transfer.confirmed', {
        status: 'confirmed',
        intentId: intent.id,
        reference: intent.bank_reference,
        approvalId: approval.id,
      });
      return execute(ctx, intent, input);
    }
    intent = byId.get(approval.intent_id, userId) as Intent;
    const used = !!(
      db.prepare('SELECT consumed_at FROM approvals WHERE 
  id=?').get(approval.id) as Approval
    ).consumed_at;
    const base = { intentId: intent.id };
    if (intent.status === 'cancelled') return { status: 'cancelled', ...base,
  message: CANCELLED_MESSAGE };
    if (!used) return { status: 'expired', ...base, message: EXPIRED_MESSAGE };
    // Already confirmed: report the outcome of that same intent. Never a second
  payment.
    if (intent.status === 'completed' && intent.operation_id) return
  completedResult(ctx, intent, input);
    if (intent.status === 'pending_verification') return recheck(ctx, intent,
  input);
    if (intent.status === 'failed')
      return {
        status: 'failed',
        error: intent.error,
        ...base,
        reference: intent.bank_reference,
        message: customerMessage(
          'resolved_not_executed',
          await describe(userId, input, intent.bank_reference!),
        ),
      };
    // Still being sent by the first confirmation: the same reference makes it 
  the same payment.
    if (['confirmed', 'processing'].includes(intent.status)) return execute(ctx,
  intent, input);
    // A used proposal whose transfer has since been proposed again authorizes 
  nothing.
    return { status: 'expired', ...base, message: EXPIRED_MESSAGE };
  }

  New: cancel

  export function cancelTransfer(userId: string, approvalId: string):
  ActionResult {
    if (person(userId)?.role !== 'customer')
      throw new HttpError(403, 'A customer account is required.');
    const db = appDb();
    const approval = db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM approvals WHERE id=? AND user_id=?')
      .get(approvalId, userId) as Approval | undefined;
    if (!approval) throw new HttpError(404, 'Proposal not found.');
    // Cancelling and confirming compete for the same single use: only one can 
  win.
    const cancelled = db.transaction(() => {
      const used =
        db
          .prepare('UPDATE approvals SET consumed_at=? WHERE id=? AND 
  consumed_at IS NULL')
          .run(new Date().toISOString(), approval.id).changes === 1;
      if (used)
        db.prepare("UPDATE intents SET status='cancelled' WHERE id=? AND 
  status='proposed'").run(
          approval.intent_id,
        );
      return used;
    })();
    const intent = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM intents WHERE 
  id=?').get(approval.intent_id) as Intent;
    if (cancelled)
      recordEvent(
        { userId, conversationId: intent.conversation_id, runId: randomUUID(),
  intentId: intent.id },
        'transfer.cancelled',
        { status: 'cancelled', intentId: intent.id, reference:
  intent.bank_reference, approvalId },
      );
    if (cancelled || intent.status === 'cancelled')
      return { status: 'cancelled', intentId: intent.id, message:
  CANCELLED_MESSAGE };
    throw new HttpError(409, 'This transfer was already confirmed and cannot be 
  cancelled.');
  }

  Refactors with no behaviour change

  - execute(ctx, intent, input): the tail of today's transferMoney (ownership
    check at execution time, processing, send with the stable reference,
    settle). One addition: if the account is no longer the customer's at
    execution time, the intent is settled as "not executed" instead of being
    left half-way.
  - completedResult(...): today's "same intent already completed" block,
    extracted because confirmation reuses it.
  - recheck(...) loses its identical parameter.

  Reconciliation query: a confirmed intent that was never sent (a crash between
  confirming and sending) is treated like a stale processing one:

  -           OR (status='processing' AND COALESCE(last_sent_at,created_at)<?3)
  +           OR (status IN ('processing','confirmed') AND 
  COALESCE(last_sent_at,created_at)<?3)

  Three wording points that differ from what you approved

  1. "Expires at {time}" in server text. The server does not know the customer's
     time zone, so a clock time in a server message can be wrong. I propose the
     server message says "expires in N minutes" (computed when returned), and
     the review card in part (c) shows the clock time from the browser, as
     approved.
  2. "Completed … at {time}" in the warning. Same reason: the server text says
     "in the last 24 hours" and carries the exact timestamp as data, so the card
     can show "at {time}".
  3. Cancelling an already confirmed transfer has no approved wording. I propose
     a 409 with "This transfer was already confirmed and cannot be cancelled."
