  - After (a), the chat tool also returns proposals, because the agent calls the
    same function. Part (b) then fixes its description, instructions and the
    write-back to the conversation.

    confirmation" panel and its Confirm button work, but a pending result would
    show the raw text pending_verification.
  - Expected after (a): R5, R15, R18, R20, R21 and R22 turn green, giving 17 of
    18, with only R10 red. R4 and R13 are the ones I will watch.

  src/banking/actions.ts: the outcome is written back to the conversation

  // What the customer did in the app, and what the bank verified, belongs in 
  the conversation
  // where the transfer was asked for: otherwise the chat ends at "please 
  confirm".
  function noteInConversation(intent: Intent, text: string) {
    if (!intent.conversation_id) return;
    appDb()
      .prepare('INSERT INTO messages VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)')
      .run(randomUUID(), intent.conversation_id, 'assistant', text, new
  Date().toISOString(), null);
  }

  It is called in three places, each only once per event:

  Two small helpers at the top of the file:

  const clock = (value: string) =>
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-IE', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit'
  }).format(new Date(value));
  const mask = (iban: string) => `${iban.slice(0, 9)} ···· ${iban.slice(-4)}`;

  Confirm and Cancel: one handler replaces confirm()

    // The customer's decision on a proposal: the only way a transfer is ever 
  sent.
    async function decide(id: string, action: 'confirm' | 'cancel') {
      const g = generation.current;
      setDeciding(id);
      setError('');
      try {
        const result = await api(`approvals/${id}/${action}`, {});
        if (g !== generation.current) return;
        // Each status in one place: a pending transfer lives in the 
  checked-transfers panel.
        if (result.status === 'pending_verification')
          setTransfers((shown) => [...shown.filter((t) => t.intentId !==
  result.intentId), result]);
        else setNotice(result.message || result.error || result.status);
        if (id === formProposal.current) {
          // O1: once confirmed, the form no longer holds a payment that one 
  click would repeat.
          if (action === 'confirm') {
            setTo('');
            setAmount('');
            setConcept('');
          }
          formProposal.current = '';
          transferIntent.current = '';
        }
        await refresh(g);
        // The outcome was written to the conversation it came from: show it 
  there too.
        const open = activeConversation.current;
        if (open) {
          const c = await api(`conversations/${open}`);
          if (g === generation.current && activeConversation.current === open)
  setMessages(c.messages);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (g === generation.current) setError((e as Error).message);
      } finally {
        if (g === generation.current) setDeciding(null);
      }
    }

  New state next to the existing refs: const [deciding, setDeciding] = 
  useState<string | null>(null), const formProposal = useRef('') and const 
  reviewCard = useRef<HTMLElement>(null). selectPerson resets them.

    // A new proposal appears below the form or the chat: bring it into view.

  'smooth', block: 'nearest' });

    // An expired proposal must not stay on screen as if it could still be 
  confirmed.
    useEffect(() => {

      const next = Math.min(...proposals.map((a) => Date.parse(a.expires_at)));
      const timer = setTimeout(() => refresh().catch(() => {}), Math.max(0, next
  - Date.now()) + 500);

    }, [dashboard]);

  What to know

  - One deviation from the approved wording. The "already completed" warning
    shows the date and the time ("at 09 Oct, 19:47"), using the app's existing
    date format, where you approved "at {time}". A transfer completed yesterday
    is still within the 24-hour window, and a bare time would be misleading. The
    expiry line shows the time only, since it is always within ten minutes.
  - The form is only cleared when the confirmed proposal came from the form.
    Confirming a proposal that came from the chat leaves anything you typed in
    the form alone. On Cancel the form keeps its data, so you can correct and
    review again.
  - Additions you did not list: scrolling the card into view, and the timer that
    refreshes when a proposal expires. Each can be dropped.
  - The overview's account cards are untouched; only the new card uses the mask
    helper.
  - I picked the warning's background colour by eye from the existing notice
    style; I have not seen it rendered.

  # Fix 2. Prueba de aceptación humana

  La hace Fernando en el navegador.

  - **Cuándo:** una vez, con Fix 2 completo (partes a, b y c).
  - **Capturas:** se guardan en `submission/notes/evidencia/aceptacion/` con el
  nombre indicado en cada paso.
  - **Garantía y modelo:** en todas las secciones, que no se mueva dinero sin
  pulsar «Confirm transfer» está garantizado por el servidor. Lo que dice el
  asistente depende del modelo y puede variar; esas secciones llevan la marca
  **(modelo)**.

  ## Preparación

  1. Parar los servicios (Ctrl+C) y ejecutar: `nvm use`, `npm run reset`, `npm
  run dev`.
  2. En otra terminal: `nvm use` y `npm run scenario -- normal`.
  3. Recargar http://127.0.0.1:3000 y abrir como Lucía. Saldo de partida de
  Aurora account: €4,007.50.
  4. No ejecutar `npm run repro` durante la prueba: restablece el banco.
  5. Vigilar la terminal de los servicios: no debe aparecer ninguna línea que
  termine en `_failed`.

  D12, to append to 02-decisions.md:

  ## D12. El resultado escrito en la conversación no se repite como aviso

  - **Fecha:** 2026-10-09.
  - **Decisión:** cuando el resultado de confirmar o cancelar una propuesta se
  escribe en la conversación que el cliente tiene abierta en pantalla, la
  interfaz no muestra además el aviso amarillo. El servidor indica en qué
  conversación lo ha escrito y la página decide. Si el cliente está en otra
  página, o la propuesta no nació en una conversación, el aviso se muestra como
  antes. En el mismo cambio se corrige la redacción de la advertencia de pago
  idéntico: «on {fecha} at {hora}».
  - **Alternativas descartadas:** dejar el resultado en los dos sitios; quitar
  siempre el aviso amarillo; dejar de escribir el resultado en la conversación.
  - **Motivo:** es el mismo principio de D10, cada estado en un solo sitio. Al
  verificar las capturas de la prueba de aceptación de Fix 2, el mismo resultado
  aparecía a la vez en el aviso y en la burbuja del chat (O5); no se
  contradecían, pero dos sitios para un mismo hecho acaban divergiendo. La
  conversación es el sitio que perdura y el que verá un operador, así que es el
  aviso el que se retira.

  What to know

  - The notice still shows when the conversation is not on screen. If you
    confirm a chat proposal from the Overview or Transfers page, you get the
    yellow notice, because the bubble is not visible there.
  - One duplication remains by design. In the pending case (your M-D-1), the
    conversation shows the pending and the "checked" bubbles, and the "Transfers
    checked with the bank" panel shows "completed, once" too. The panel is the
    place that follows the customer across pages (D10), so I left it. Say so if
    you want that treated as well.
  - A repeated confirmation of the same proposal still shows the notice, since
    nothing new is written to the conversation.
